    22→  <dst-uri>/shards/shard_NNN/tokens.bin + tokens.idx + metadata.json
    23→
    24→  Shards are numbered globally across all coreset batches (sequential mode).
    25→  Each shard's metadata.json carries source_file for full traceability.
    26→
    27→Features:
    28→  - Multi-File Support: Processes a single coreset file OR a folder of parquet files.
    29→  - Per-File Isolation: Each coreset file gets its own output subfolder.
    30→  - Manifest-Driven: Only includes specific chunks listed in the coreset.
    31→  - Standard Output: 512 MB shards with spdl-compatible binary .idx.

    33→    stage, row counts, drop stats, and created_at timestamp.

    97→                    termination_time = resp.read().decode().strip()

     2→"""
     3→Flatten Shards — Post-Processing Step for Curriculum Tokenizer
     4→==============================================================
     5→
     6→Converts the per-batch staging layout produced by tokenize_curriculum.py
     7→into a flat, globally-numbered shard directory suitable for training pipelines.
     8→
     9→Staging layout (written by tokenize_curriculum.py):
    10→  <src-dir>/
    11→    selected_indices_small_batch000000/
    12→      shard_001/ -> tokens.bin, tokens.idx, metadata.json
    13→      shard_002/ -> ...
    14→    selected_indices_small_batch000001/
    15→      shard_001/ -> ...
    16→      shard_002/ -> ...
    17→    ...
    18→
    19→Output layout (written by this script):
    20→  <dst-dir>/
    21→    shards/
    22→      shard_001/ -> tokens.bin, tokens.idx, metadata.json  (shard_name patched)
    23→      shard_002/ -> ...
    24→      shard_003/ -> ...        <- first shard of second batch, renumbered
    25→      ...
    26→    flatten_manifest.json      <- batch -> global shard range mapping
    27→
    28→Design decisions:
    29→  - Resumable: shards already present in <dst-dir>/shards/ are skipped.
    30→  - Streaming: copy shard N -> delete staging shard N before moving to N+1,
    31→    so disk/S3 never holds more than one extra shard at a time.
    32→  - metadata.json patched in-place: only shard_name changes; all other fields
    33→    (tokenizer_hash, source_file, band, domain, ...) are preserved.
    34→  - Deterministic ordering: batches sorted lexicographically; shards within
    35→    each batch sorted by their original shard_NNN name.
    36→  - Local runs use os.rename (O(1) inode update, zero data movement).
    37→  - S3 runs use CopyObject + DeleteObject (server-side, no egress cost).
    38→
    39→Usage:
    40→  # Local
    41→  python flatten_shards.py \\
    42→      --src-dir ./tok_staging \\
    43→      --dst-dir ./output
    44→
    45→  # S3
    46→  python flatten_shards.py \\
    47→      --src-uri s3://my-bucket/tok_staging \\
    48→      --dst-uri s3://my-bucket/output
    49→
    50→  # Dry-run (print plan, no writes)
    51→  python flatten_shards.py --src-dir ./tok_staging --dst-dir ./output --dry-run
    52→
    53→  # Resume (skip already-present shards in dst)
    54→  python flatten_shards.py --src-dir ./tok_staging --dst-dir ./output
    55→  # (resumable by default — safe to re-run)
    56→"""
    57→
    58→from __future__ import annotations
    59→
    60→import argparse
    61→import json
    62→import os
    63→import shutil
    64→import struct
    65→import sys
    66→import tempfile
    67→from datetime import datetime, timezone

    69→from urllib.parse import urlparse

    71→
    72→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    73→# Constants

    76→SHARD_FILES = ("tokens.bin", "tokens.idx", "metadata.json")
    77→
    78→
    79→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    80→# URI helpers

    82→
    83→
    84→def is_s3_uri(uri: str) -> bool:
    85→    return uri.startswith("s3://")
    86→
    87→
    88→def parse_s3_url(url: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    89→    parsed = urlparse(url)
    90→    if parsed.scheme != "s3":
    91→        raise ValueError(f"Not an S3 URI: {url}")
    92→    return parsed.netloc, parsed.path.lstrip("/")
    93→
    94→
    95→def _s3_key_join(prefix: str, *parts: str) -> str:
    96→    """Join S3 key segments, normalising slashes."""
    97→    result = prefix.rstrip("/")
    98→    for part in parts:
    99→        result = result.rstrip("/") + "/" + part.lstrip("/")
   100→    return result
   101→
   102→
   103→def _s3_uri_join(base_uri: str, *parts: str) -> str:
   104→    """Join an S3 URI with path segments."""
   105→    bucket, prefix = parse_s3_url(base_uri)
   106→    key = _s3_key_join(prefix, *parts)
   107→    return f"s3://{bucket}/{key}"
   108→
   109→
   110→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111→# S3 operations
   112→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   113→
   114→
   115→def _get_s3():
   116→    import boto3
   117→    return boto3.client("s3")
   118→
   119→
   120→def s3_key_exists(s3, bucket: str, key: str) -> bool:
   121→    from botocore.exceptions import ClientError
   122→    try:
   123→        s3.head_object(Bucket=bucket, Key=key)
   124→        return True
   125→    except ClientError as e:
   126→        if e.response["Error"]["Code"] in ("404", "NoSuchKey"):
   127→            return False
   128→        raise
   129→
   130→
   131→def s3_list_immediate_subdirs(s3, bucket: str, prefix: str) -> List[str]:
   132→    """Return sorted list of immediate sub-prefixes under prefix (one level deep)."""
   133→    prefix = prefix.rstrip("/") + "/"
   134→    paginator = s3.get_paginator("list_objects_v2")
   135→    subdirs = set()
   136→    for page in paginator.paginate(Bucket=bucket, Prefix=prefix, Delimiter="/"):
   137→        for cp in page.get("CommonPrefixes", []):
   138→            # cp["Prefix"] looks like "prefix/subdir/"
   139→            rel = cp["Prefix"][len(prefix):].rstrip("/")
   140→            if rel:
   141→                subdirs.add(rel)
   142→    return sorted(subdirs)
   143→
   144→
   145→def s3_list_objects_under(s3, bucket: str, prefix: str) -> List[str]:
   146→    """Return all object keys under prefix."""
   147→    prefix = prefix.rstrip("/") + "/"
   148→    paginator = s3.get_paginator("list_objects_v2")
   149→    keys = []
   150→    for page in paginator.paginate(Bucket=bucket, Prefix=prefix):
   151→        for obj in page.get("Contents", []):
   152→            keys.append(obj["Key"])
   153→    return keys
   154→
   155→
   156→def s3_copy_object(s3, src_bucket: str, src_key: str, dst_bucket: str, dst_key: str) -> None:
   157→    s3.copy_object(
   158→        CopySource={"Bucket": src_bucket, "Key": src_key},
   159→        Bucket=dst_bucket,
   160→        Key=dst_key,
   161→    )
   162→
   163→
   164→def s3_delete_object(s3, bucket: str, key: str) -> None:
   165→    s3.delete_object(Bucket=bucket, Key=key)
   166→
   167→
   168→def s3_read_json(s3, bucket: str, key: str) -> dict:
   169→    resp = s3.get_object(Bucket=bucket, Key=key)
   170→    return json.loads(resp["Body"].read().decode("utf-8"))
   171→
   172→
   173→def s3_put_json(s3, bucket: str, key: str, data: dict) -> None:
   174→    body = json.dumps(data, indent=2).encode("utf-8")
   175→    s3.put_object(Bucket=bucket, Key=key, Body=body, ContentType="application/json")
   176→
   177→
   178→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→# Staging layout discovery
   180→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181→
   182→
   183→def discover_staging_batches_local(src_dir: str) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
   184→    """
   185→    Walk src_dir and return (batch_name, batch_path) pairs sorted by batch_name.
   186→    A batch directory is any immediate subdir of src_dir that itself contains
   187→    at least one shard_NNN/ subdirectory.
   188→    """
   189→    if not os.path.isdir(src_dir):
   190→        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source directory not found: {src_dir}")
   191→
   192→    batches = []
   193→    for entry in sorted(os.listdir(src_dir)):
   194→        batch_path = os.path.join(src_dir, entry)
   195→        if not os.path.isdir(batch_path):
   196→            continue
   197→        # Check it has at least one shard_NNN subdir
   198→        has_shards = any(
   199→            sub.startswith("shard_") and os.path.isdir(os.path.join(batch_path, sub))
   200→            for sub in os.listdir(batch_path)
   201→        )
   202→        if has_shards:
   203→            batches.append((entry, batch_path))
   204→    return batches
   205→
   206→
   207→def discover_staging_batches_s3(s3, src_bucket: str, src_prefix: str) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
   208→    """
   209→    Return (batch_name, full_s3_prefix) pairs from S3, sorted by batch_name.
   210→    """
   211→    batch_names = s3_list_immediate_subdirs(s3, src_bucket, src_prefix)
   212→    result = []
   213→    for name in batch_names:
   214→        prefix = _s3_key_join(src_prefix, name)
   215→        # Verify it contains shard subdirs
   216→        shard_subdirs = [
   217→            d for d in s3_list_immediate_subdirs(s3, src_bucket, prefix)
   218→            if d.startswith("shard_")
   219→        ]
   220→        if shard_subdirs:
   221→            result.append((name, f"s3://{src_bucket}/{prefix}"))
   222→    return result
   223→
   224→
   225→def list_shard_dirs_in_batch_local(batch_path: str) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
   226→    """Return sorted (shard_name, shard_path) pairs inside a local batch dir."""
   227→    shards = []
   228→    for sub in sorted(os.listdir(batch_path)):
   229→        if sub.startswith("shard_") and os.path.isdir(os.path.join(batch_path, sub)):
   230→            shards.append((sub, os.path.join(batch_path, sub)))
   231→    return shards
   232→
   233→
   234→def list_shard_dirs_in_batch_s3(s3, src_bucket: str, batch_prefix: str) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
   235→    """Return sorted (shard_name, s3_prefix) pairs inside an S3 batch prefix."""
   236→    shard_names = sorted([
   237→        d for d in s3_list_immediate_subdirs(s3, src_bucket, batch_prefix)
   238→        if d.startswith("shard_")
   239→    ])
   240→    return [
   241→        (name, f"s3://{src_bucket}/{_s3_key_join(batch_prefix, name)}")
   242→        for name in shard_names
   243→    ]
   244→
   245→
   246→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   247→# Core flatten operations
   248→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   249→
   250→
   251→def _patch_metadata_shard_name(meta: dict, new_shard_name: str) -> dict:
   252→    """Return a copy of metadata with shard_name updated to new_shard_name."""
   253→    patched = dict(meta)
   254→    patched["shard_name"] = new_shard_name
   255→    return patched
   256→
   257→
   258→def flatten_shard_local(
   259→    src_shard_dir: str,
   260→    dst_shard_dir: str,
   261→    new_shard_name: str,
   262→    dry_run: bool = False,
   263→) -> None:
   264→    """
   265→    Move a shard from staging to flat output directory (local).
   266→
   267→    Strategy: rename the directory (O(1) if on same filesystem), then patch
   268→    metadata.json in-place. Falls back to copy+delete if src and dst are on
   269→    different filesystems.
   270→    """
   271→    if dry_run:
   272→        print(f"    [DRY-RUN] {src_shard_dir} -> {dst_shard_dir} (shard_name={new_shard_name})")
   273→        return
   274→
   275→    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst_shard_dir), exist_ok=True)
   276→
   277→    try:
   278→        os.rename(src_shard_dir, dst_shard_dir)
   279→    except OSError:
   280→        # Cross-device: fall back to copy then delete
   281→        shutil.copytree(src_shard_dir, dst_shard_dir)
   282→        shutil.rmtree(src_shard_dir)
   283→
   284→    # Patch metadata.json in-place
   285→    meta_path = os.path.join(dst_shard_dir, "metadata.json")
   286→    if os.path.exists(meta_path):
   287→        with open(meta_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   288→            meta = json.load(f)
   289→        meta = _patch_metadata_shard_name(meta, new_shard_name)
   290→        with open(meta_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   291→            json.dump(meta, f, indent=2)
   292→
   293→
   294→def flatten_shard_s3(
   295→    s3,
   296→    src_bucket: str,
   297→    src_prefix: str,
   298→    dst_bucket: str,
   299→    dst_prefix: str,
   300→    new_shard_name: str,
   301→    dry_run: bool = False,
   302→) -> None:
   303→    """
   304→    Move a shard from staging to flat output on S3 (CopyObject + DeleteObject).
   305→
   306→    Streaming strategy: copy each file, then immediately delete its source.
   307→    metadata.json is patched (GET -> modify -> PUT) before the source is deleted.
   308→    """
   309→    src_prefix = src_prefix.rstrip("/")
   310→    dst_prefix = dst_prefix.rstrip("/")
   311→
   312→    for fname in SHARD_FILES:
   313→        src_key = f"{src_prefix}/{fname}"
   314→        dst_key = f"{dst_prefix}/{fname}"
   315→
   316→        if dry_run:
   317→            print(f"    [DRY-RUN] s3://{src_bucket}/{src_key} -> s3://{dst_bucket}/{dst_key}")
   318→            continue
   319→
   320→        if fname == "metadata.json":
   321→            # Patch shard_name before writing to destination
   322→            meta = s3_read_json(s3, src_bucket, src_key)
   323→            meta = _patch_metadata_shard_name(meta, new_shard_name)
   324→            s3_put_json(s3, dst_bucket, dst_key, meta)
   325→        else:
   326→            s3_copy_object(s3, src_bucket, src_key, dst_bucket, dst_key)
   327→
   328→        # Delete source immediately (streaming — never hold both at once)
   329→        s3_delete_object(s3, src_bucket, src_key)
   330→
   331→
   332→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   333→# Flatten manifest
   334→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   335→
   336→
   337→def write_flatten_manifest_local(dst_dir: str, manifest: dict) -> None:
   338→    path = os.path.join(dst_dir, "flatten_manifest.json")
   339→    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   340→        json.dump(manifest, f, indent=2)
   341→    print(f"\nSaved flatten_manifest.json -> {path}")
   342→
   343→
   344→def write_flatten_manifest_s3(s3, dst_bucket: str, dst_prefix: str, manifest: dict) -> None:
   345→    key = _s3_key_join(dst_prefix, "flatten_manifest.json")
   346→    s3_put_json(s3, dst_bucket, key, manifest)
   347→    print(f"\nSaved flatten_manifest.json -> s3://{dst_bucket}/{key}")
   348→
   349→
   350→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   351→# Orchestrator
   352→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   353→
   354→
   355→def run_flatten(
   356→    src: str,
   357→    dst: str,
   358→    s3_client=None,
   359→    dry_run: bool = False,
   360→    fail_fast: bool = False,
   361→) -> dict:
   362→    """
   363→    Flatten per-batch staging shards into a global flat shards/ directory.
   364→
   365→    Can be called as a library function from tokenize_curriculum.py or via CLI.
   366→
   367→    Args:
   368→        src: Local path or S3 URI of the staging directory
   369→             (contains <coreset_name>/shard_NNN/ subdirs).
   370→        dst: Local path or S3 URI of the output directory
   371→             (shards/ will be created under it).
   372→        s3_client: Optional pre-created boto3 S3 client. Created automatically
   373→                   if None and src/dst are S3 URIs.
   374→        dry_run: If True, print the plan without making any changes.
   375→        fail_fast: If True, stop on the first shard error.
   376→
   377→    Returns:
   378→        dict with keys: total_shards_moved, failed, manifest_entries
   379→    """
   380→    src_is_s3 = is_s3_uri(src)
   381→    dst_is_s3 = is_s3_uri(dst)
   382→
   383→    s3 = s3_client
   384→    if (src_is_s3 or dst_is_s3) and s3 is None:
   385→        s3 = _get_s3()
   386→
   387→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   388→    # Resolve source / destination
   389→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   390→    if src_is_s3:
   391→        src_bucket, src_prefix = parse_s3_url(src)
   392→    else:
   393→        src_dir = src
   394→
   395→    if dst_is_s3:
   396→        dst_bucket, dst_prefix = parse_s3_url(dst)
   397→        # Output shards go under <dst>/shards/
   398→        shards_prefix = _s3_key_join(dst_prefix, "shards")
   399→    else:
   400→        dst_dir = dst
   401→        shards_dir = os.path.join(dst_dir, "shards")
   402→        os.makedirs(shards_dir, exist_ok=True)
   403→
   404→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   405→    # Discover staging batches
   406→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   407→    print("Discovering staging batches...")
   408→    if src_is_s3:
   409→        batches = discover_staging_batches_s3(s3, src_bucket, src_prefix)
   410→    else:
   411→        batches = discover_staging_batches_local(src_dir)
   412→
   413→    if not batches:
   414→        print("No batch directories with shards found. Nothing to do.")
   415→        return {"total_shards_moved": 0, "failed": 0, "manifest_entries": {}}
   416→
   417→    print(f"Found {len(batches)} batch(es).")
   418→
   419→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   420→    # Determine start index (resume support)
   421→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   422→    # Count how many shards already exist in the destination
   423→    start_global_idx = 1
   424→    if dst_is_s3:
   425→        existing = sorted([
   426→            d for d in s3_list_immediate_subdirs(s3, dst_bucket, shards_prefix)
   427→            if d.startswith("shard_")
   428→        ])
   429→    else:
   430→        if os.path.isdir(shards_dir):
   431→            existing = sorted([
   432→                d for d in os.listdir(shards_dir)
   433→                if d.startswith("shard_") and os.path.isdir(os.path.join(shards_dir, d))
   434→            ])
   435→        else:
   436→            existing = []
   437→
   438→    if existing:
   439→        # Find the highest existing global index
   440→        last_idx = max(int(d.split("_")[1]) for d in existing)
   441→        start_global_idx = last_idx + 1
   442→        print(f"Resuming: {len(existing)} shard(s) already in destination. "
   443→              f"Next global index: {start_global_idx}.")
   444→
   445→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   446→    # Walk all batches and build the plan
   447→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   448→    # plan: list of (batch_name, old_shard_name, src_shard_path_or_prefix, global_idx)
   449→    plan: List[Tuple[str, str, str, int]] = []
   450→    global_idx = start_global_idx
   451→
   452→    for batch_name, batch_path_or_prefix in batches:
   453→        if src_is_s3:
   454→            batch_prefix_key = parse_s3_url(batch_path_or_prefix)[1]
   455→            shards_in_batch = list_shard_dirs_in_batch_s3(s3, src_bucket, batch_prefix_key)
   456→        else:
   457→            shards_in_batch = list_shard_dirs_in_batch_local(batch_path_or_prefix)
   458→
   459→        for old_shard_name, shard_path_or_prefix in shards_in_batch:
   460→            plan.append((batch_name, old_shard_name, shard_path_or_prefix, global_idx))
   461→            global_idx += 1
   462→
   463→    total_shards = len(plan)
   464→    print(f"Total shards to move: {total_shards}")
   465→    if total_shards == 0:
   466→        print("Nothing to flatten (all shards may already be in destination).")
   467→        return {"total_shards_moved": 0, "failed": 0, "manifest_entries": {}}
   468→
   469→    if dry_run:
   470→        print("\n[DRY-RUN MODE] No files will be written.\n")
   471→
   472→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   473→    # Execute flatten
   474→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   475→    # manifest_entries: per-batch shard range info
   476→    manifest_entries: Dict[str, dict] = {}
   477→    processed = 0
   478→    failed = 0
   479→
   480→    for batch_name, old_shard_name, src_path, gidx in plan:
   481→        new_shard_name = f"shard_{gidx:03d}"
   482→
   483→        if dst_is_s3:
   484→            dst_shard_prefix = _s3_key_join(shards_prefix, new_shard_name)
   485→        else:
   486→            dst_shard_dir = os.path.join(shards_dir, new_shard_name)
   487→
   488→        print(f"  [{processed+1}/{total_shards}] {batch_name}/{old_shard_name} -> {new_shard_name}")
   489→
   490→        try:
   491→            if src_is_s3 and dst_is_s3:
   492→                src_shard_prefix_key = parse_s3_url(src_path)[1]
   493→                flatten_shard_s3(
   494→                    s3,
   495→                    src_bucket, src_shard_prefix_key,
   496→                    dst_bucket, _s3_key_join(shards_prefix, new_shard_name),
   497→                    new_shard_name,
   498→                    dry_run=dry_run,
   499→                )
   500→            elif not src_is_s3 and not dst_is_s3:
   501→                flatten_shard_local(src_path, dst_shard_dir, new_shard_name, dry_run=dry_run)
   502→            else:
   503→                raise NotImplementedError(
   504→                    "Mixed local-source and S3-destination (or vice versa) is not supported."
   505→                )
   506→
   507→            # Track per-batch shard range for manifest
   508→            if batch_name not in manifest_entries:
   509→                manifest_entries[batch_name] = {"shard_start": gidx, "shard_end": gidx}
   510→            else:
   511→                manifest_entries[batch_name]["shard_end"] = gidx
   512→
   513→            processed += 1
   514→
   515→        except Exception as e:
   516→            print(f"    ERROR: {e}")
   517→            failed += 1
   518→            if fail_fast:
   519→                print("Stopping due to fail_fast.")
   520→                break
   521→
   522→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   523→    # Write flatten_manifest.json
   524→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   525→    manifest = {
   526→        "format": "flatten_manifest_v1",
   527→        "src": src,
   528→        "dst": dst,
   529→        "total_shards_moved": processed,
   530→        "batches": [
   531→            {
   532→                "batch_name": name,
   533→                "shard_start": info["shard_start"],
   534→                "shard_end": info["shard_end"],
   535→                "num_shards": info["shard_end"] - info["shard_start"] + 1,
   536→            }
   537→            for name, info in manifest_entries.items()
   538→        ],
   539→        "created_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
   540→    }
   541→
   542→    if not dry_run:
   543→        if dst_is_s3:
   544→            write_flatten_manifest_s3(s3, dst_bucket, dst_prefix, manifest)
   545→        else:
   546→            write_flatten_manifest_local(dst_dir, manifest)
   547→
   548→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   549→    # Summary
   550→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   551→    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
   552→    print(f"Flatten complete: {processed}/{total_shards} shards moved.")
   553→    if failed:
   554→        print(f"  WARNING: {failed} shard(s) failed.")
   555→    if dry_run:
   556→        print("  (DRY-RUN — no files were written)")
   557→    print("=" * 60)
   558→
   559→    return {"total_shards_moved": processed, "failed": failed, "manifest_entries": manifest_entries}
   560→
   561→
   562→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   563→# CLI
   564→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   565→
   566→
   567→def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
   568→    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
   569→        description="Flatten per-batch staging shards into a global flat shards/ directory.",
   570→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,

   611→

     3→Shard Validation Script

     5→
     6→Validates tokenized shard directories against the 8-point checklist defined in

     8→
     9→Checks per shard:
    10→  1. Required files present: tokens.bin, tokens.idx, metadata.json
    11→  2. tokenizer_hash matches the current canonical tokenizer

    53→def parse_s3_url(url: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    54→    parsed = urlparse(url)
    55→    return parsed.netloc, parsed.path.lstrip("/")

    63→def key_exists_local(path: str) -> bool:

    67→def read_bytes_local(path: str) -> bytes:
    68→    with open(path, "rb") as f:
    69→        return f.read()

    71→
    72→def read_json_local(path: str) -> dict:
    73→    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:

    77→def list_shard_dirs_local(base_dir: str) -> List[str]:
    78→    """Return sorted list of shard_NNN subdirectories under <base_dir>/shards/.
    79→
    80→    Layout: <base_dir>/shards/shard_NNN/
    81→    """
    82→    shards_dir = os.path.join(base_dir, "shards")
    83→    if not os.path.isdir(shards_dir):
    84→        return []
    85→    return sorted([
    86→        os.path.join(shards_dir, d)
    87→        for d in os.listdir(shards_dir)
    88→        if d.startswith("shard_") and os.path.isdir(os.path.join(shards_dir, d))
    89→    ])

    92→def compute_tokenizer_hash(tokenizer_dir: str) -> str:
    93→    files = sorted(["special_tokens_map.json", "tokenizer.json"])
    94→    h = hashlib.sha256()
    95→    for fname in files:
    96→        fpath = os.path.join(tokenizer_dir, fname)
    97→        with open(fpath, "rb") as f:
    98→            h.update(fname.encode())
    99→            h.update(f.read())
   100→    return h.hexdigest()
   101→
   102→
   103→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   104→# Shard Validator
   105→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106→
   107→SPDL_IDX_HEADER_FMT = "<II"
   108→TOKEN_DTYPE = np.uint32
   109→BYTES_PER_TOKEN = 4
   110→
   111→
   112→def validate_shard(
   113→    shard_dir: str,
   114→    expected_tokenizer_hash: str,
   115→    live_vocab_size: int,
   116→    live_eos_token_id: int,
   117→    live_pad_token_id: int,
   118→    verbose: bool = False,
   119→) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:
   120→    """
   121→    Validate a single shard directory.
   122→
   123→    Returns (passed: bool, errors: List[str]).
   124→    """
   125→    errors = []
   126→
   127→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   128→    # Check 1: Required files present
   129→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   130→    bin_path = os.path.join(shard_dir, "tokens.bin")
   131→    idx_path = os.path.join(shard_dir, "tokens.idx")
   132→    meta_path = os.path.join(shard_dir, "metadata.json")
   133→
   134→    for path, label in [(bin_path, "tokens.bin"), (idx_path, "tokens.idx"), (meta_path, "metadata.json")]:
   135→        if not os.path.exists(path):
   136→            errors.append(f"[CHECK 1] Missing file: {label}")
   137→
   138→    if errors:
   139→        return False, errors
   140→
   141→    # Load metadata
   142→    try:
   143→        meta = read_json_local(meta_path)
   144→    except Exception as e:
   145→        errors.append(f"[CHECK 1] Cannot parse metadata.json: {e}")
   146→        return False, errors
   147→
   148→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   149→    # Check 2: tokenizer_hash matches canonical tokenizer
   150→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   151→    meta_hash = meta.get("tokenizer_hash", "")
   152→    if not meta_hash:
   153→        errors.append("[CHECK 2] tokenizer_hash is missing from metadata.json")
   154→    elif meta_hash != expected_tokenizer_hash:
   155→        errors.append(
   156→            f"[CHECK 2] tokenizer_hash mismatch: "
   157→            f"metadata={meta_hash[:16]}... expected={expected_tokenizer_hash[:16]}..."
   158→        )
   159→
   160→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   161→    # Check 3: eos_token_id, pad_token_id, vocab_size match live tokenizer
   162→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   163→    meta_eos = meta.get("eos_token_id")
   164→    meta_pad = meta.get("pad_token_id")
   165→    meta_vocab = meta.get("vocab_size")
   166→
   167→    if meta_eos != live_eos_token_id:
   168→        errors.append(f"[CHECK 3] eos_token_id mismatch: metadata={meta_eos} live={live_eos_token_id}")
   169→    if meta_pad != live_pad_token_id:
   170→        errors.append(f"[CHECK 3] pad_token_id mismatch: metadata={meta_pad} live={live_pad_token_id}")
   171→    if meta_vocab != live_vocab_size:
   172→        errors.append(f"[CHECK 3] vocab_size mismatch: metadata={meta_vocab} live={live_vocab_size}")
   173→
   174→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   175→    # Check 4: total_tokens == file_size / 4
   176→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   177→    bin_size = os.path.getsize(bin_path)
   178→    expected_tokens = bin_size // BYTES_PER_TOKEN
   179→    meta_total_tokens = meta.get("total_tokens", -1)
   180→
   181→    if meta_total_tokens != expected_tokens:
   182→        errors.append(
   183→            f"[CHECK 4] total_tokens mismatch: metadata={meta_total_tokens} "
   184→            f"computed_from_file={expected_tokens} (bin_size={bin_size})"
   185→        )
   186→
   187→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   188→    # Check 5: len(idx_offsets) - 1 == num_blocks
   189→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   190→    try:
   191→        idx_bytes = read_bytes_local(idx_path)
   192→        header_size = struct.calcsize(SPDL_IDX_HEADER_FMT)
   193→        ver, dtype_size = struct.unpack_from(SPDL_IDX_HEADER_FMT, idx_bytes, 0)
   194→        offsets = np.frombuffer(idx_bytes[header_size:], dtype=np.uint64)
   195→        num_blocks_from_idx = len(offsets) - 1
   196→        meta_num_blocks = meta.get("num_blocks", -1)
   197→
   198→        if num_blocks_from_idx != meta_num_blocks:
   199→            errors.append(
   200→                f"[CHECK 5] num_blocks mismatch: idx_file={num_blocks_from_idx} "
   201→                f"metadata={meta_num_blocks}"
   202→            )
   203→    except Exception as e:
   204→        errors.append(f"[CHECK 5] Cannot parse tokens.idx: {e}")
   205→
   206→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   207→    # Check 6: rows_dropped + rows_with_eos == rows_input
   208→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   209→    rows_input = meta.get("rows_input")
   210→    rows_with_eos = meta.get("rows_with_eos")
   211→    rows_dropped = meta.get("rows_dropped")
   212→
   213→    if rows_input is None or rows_with_eos is None or rows_dropped is None:
   214→        errors.append("[CHECK 6] Missing row audit fields: rows_input, rows_with_eos, or rows_dropped")
   215→    else:
   216→        if rows_with_eos + rows_dropped != rows_input:
   217→            errors.append(
   218→                f"[CHECK 6] Row count invariant broken: "
   219→                f"rows_with_eos({rows_with_eos}) + rows_dropped({rows_dropped}) "
   220→                f"!= rows_input({rows_input})"
   221→            )
   222→
   223→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   224→    # Check 7: max(token_ids) < vocab_size
   225→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   226→    try:
   227→        tokens = np.fromfile(bin_path, dtype=TOKEN_DTYPE)
   228→        if len(tokens) == 0:
   229→            errors.append("[CHECK 7] tokens.bin is empty")
   230→        else:
   231→            max_id = int(tokens.max())
   232→            if max_id >= live_vocab_size:
   233→                errors.append(
   234→                    f"[CHECK 7] Token ID out of vocab range: max_id={max_id} vocab_size={live_vocab_size}"
   235→                )
   236→            elif verbose:
   237→                print(f"        max_token_id={max_id}, eos_count={(tokens == live_eos_token_id).sum()}, "
   238→                      f"pad_count={(tokens == live_pad_token_id).sum()}")
   239→    except Exception as e:
   240→        errors.append(f"[CHECK 7] Cannot read tokens.bin: {e}")
   241→
   242→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   243→    # Check 8: band, domain, stage are non-empty
   244→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   245→    for field in ("band", "domain", "stage"):
   246→        val = meta.get(field)
   247→        if val is None or val == "":
   248→            errors.append(f"[CHECK 8] Required curriculum field '{field}' is missing or empty")
   249→
   250→    passed = len(errors) == 0
   251→    return passed, errors
   252→
   253→
   254→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   255→# Main
   256→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   257→
   258→
   259→def parse_args():
   260→    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Validate tokenized shard directories")
   261→    p.add_argument(
   262→        "--shards-dir",
   263→        default=None,
   264→        help="Local output directory produced by flatten_shards.py (must contain a shards/ subdir)",
   265→    )
   266→    p.add_argument(
   267→        "--tokenizer-path",
   268→        required=True,
   269→        help="Path to tokenizer directory (used to compute expected hash)",

   272→        "--verbose", action="store_true", help="Print per-check details even for passing shards"
   273→    )
   274→    p.add_argument(
   275→        "--fail-fast", action="store_true", help="Stop after the first failing shard"
   276→    )
   277→    return p.parse_args()
   278→
   279→
   280→def main():
   281→    args = parse_args()
   282→
   283→    if not args.shards_dir:
   284→        print("ERROR: --shards-dir is required.", file=sys.stderr)
   285→        sys.exit(1)
   286→
   287→    # Load live tokenizer for ground-truth token IDs
   288→    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
   289→    print(f"Loading tokenizer from {args.tokenizer_path} ...")
   290→    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.tokenizer_path)
   291→    live_vocab_size = len(tokenizer)
   292→    live_eos_token_id = tokenizer.eos_token_id
   293→    live_pad_token_id = tokenizer.pad_token_id if tokenizer.pad_token_id is not None else 0

   299→    print()
   300→
   301→    shard_dirs = list_shard_dirs_local(args.shards_dir)
   302→    if not shard_dirs:
   303→        print(f"No shard directories found under {args.shards_dir}/shards/")
   304→        print("Run flatten_shards.py first to produce the flat shards/ layout.")
   305→        sys.exit(1)
   306→
   307→    print(f"Validating {len(shard_dirs)} shard(s)...\n")
   308→
   309→    total = 0
   310→    passed = 0
   311→    failed = 0
   312→

   321→            live_pad_token_id=live_pad_token_id,
   322→            verbose=args.verbose,
   323→        )
   324→
   325→        if ok:
   326→            passed += 1
   327→            if args.verbose:
   328→                print(f"  [PASS] {rel}")
   329→        else:
   330→            failed += 1
   331→            print(f"  [FAIL] {rel}")
   332→            for err in errors:
   333→                print(f"         {err}")
   334→            if args.fail_fast:
   335→                break
   336→
   337→    print()
   338→    print("=" * 60)
   339→    print(f"Validation complete: {passed}/{total} passed, {failed} failed")
   340→    print("=" * 60)
   341→    sys.exit(0 if failed == 0 else 1)
   342→
   343→
   344→if __name__ == "__main__":
   345→    main()
   346→

   101→        except Exception:

   105→
   106→def register_interrupt_handlers():
   107→    """Register SIGTERM/SIGINT handlers and start IMDS polling thread."""
   108→    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, _handle_sigterm)
   109→    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, _handle_sigterm)
   110→    t = threading.Thread(target=_poll_spot_termination, daemon=True)
   111→    t.start()

   114→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→# S3 / Local File Helpers
   116→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→
   119→def is_s3_uri(uri: str) -> bool:
   120→    return uri.startswith("s3://")
   121→
   122→
   123→def parse_s3_url(url: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
   124→    """Parse s3://bucket/key -> (bucket, key)."""
   125→    parsed = urlparse(url)
   126→    if parsed.scheme != "s3":
   127→        raise ValueError(f"Invalid S3 URL: {url}")
   128→    return parsed.netloc, parsed.path.lstrip("/")
   129→
   130→
   131→def download_to_temp(s3, uri: str, tmp_dir: str) -> str:
   132→    """Download an S3 object to a local temp file, or return local path if already local."""
   133→    if is_s3_uri(uri):
   134→        if s3 is None:
   135→            import boto3
   136→            s3 = boto3.client("s3")
   137→        bucket, key = parse_s3_url(uri)
   138→        basename = os.path.basename(key)
   139→        local_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, basename)
   140→        s3.download_file(bucket, key, local_path)
   141→        return local_path
   142→    return uri
   143→
   144→
   145→def upload_file(s3, local_path: str, dst_uri: str) -> None:
   146→    """Upload a local file to S3 or copy to local destination."""
   147→    if is_s3_uri(dst_uri):
   148→        if s3 is None:
   149→            import boto3
   150→            s3 = boto3.client("s3")
   151→        bucket, key = parse_s3_url(dst_uri)
   152→        s3.upload_file(local_path, bucket, key)
   153→    else:
   154→        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst_uri), exist_ok=True)
   155→        shutil.copy2(local_path, dst_uri)
   156→
   157→
   158→def key_exists(s3, uri: str) -> bool:
   159→    """Check if an S3 key or local file/dir exists."""
   160→    if is_s3_uri(uri):
   161→        if s3 is None:
   162→            import boto3
   163→            s3 = boto3.client("s3")
   164→        from botocore.exceptions import ClientError
   165→        bucket, key = parse_s3_url(uri)
   166→        try:
   167→            s3.head_object(Bucket=bucket, Key=key)
   168→            return True
   169→        except ClientError as e:
   170→            if e.response["Error"]["Code"] in ("404", "NoSuchKey"):
   171→                return False
   172→            raise
   173→    return os.path.exists(uri)
   174→
   175→
   176→def delete_s3_prefix(s3, prefix_uri: str) -> None:
   177→    """Delete all S3 objects under a given prefix URI."""
   178→    if s3 is None:
   179→        import boto3
   180→        s3 = boto3.client("s3")
   181→    bucket, prefix = parse_s3_url(prefix_uri)
   182→    prefix = prefix.rstrip("/") + "/"
   183→    paginator = s3.get_paginator("list_objects_v2")
   184→    for page in paginator.paginate(Bucket=bucket, Prefix=prefix):
   185→        objects = [{"Key": obj["Key"]} for obj in page.get("Contents", [])]
   186→        if objects:
   187→            s3.delete_objects(Bucket=bucket, Delete={"Objects": objects})
   188→
   189→
   190→def list_parquet_files(s3, uri: str) -> List[str]:
   191→    """List all .parquet files under an S3 prefix or a local folder."""
   192→    files = []
   193→    if is_s3_uri(uri):
   194→        if s3 is None:
   195→            import boto3
   196→            s3 = boto3.client("s3")
   197→        bucket, prefix = parse_s3_url(uri)
   198→        paginator = s3.get_paginator("list_objects_v2")
   199→        for page in paginator.paginate(Bucket=bucket, Prefix=prefix):
   200→            for obj in page.get("Contents", []):
   201→                key = obj["Key"]
   202→                if key.endswith(".parquet"):
   203→                    files.append(f"s3://{bucket}/{key}")
   204→    else:
   205→        if os.path.isfile(uri) and uri.endswith(".parquet"):
   206→            files.append(uri)
   207→        elif os.path.isdir(uri):
   208→            for root, _, filenames in os.walk(uri):
   209→                for filename in filenames:
   210→                    if filename.endswith(".parquet"):
   211→                        files.append(os.path.join(root, filename).replace("\\", "/"))
   212→    return files
   213→
   214→
   215→def upload_json(s3, data: dict, dst_uri: str, tmp_dir: str) -> None:
   216→    """Write a dict as JSON and upload to S3 or local path."""
   217→    tmp_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "_tmp_upload.json")
   218→    with open(tmp_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   219→        json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
   220→    upload_file(s3, tmp_path, dst_uri)
   221→    os.remove(tmp_path)
   222→
   223→
   224→def download_json(s3, uri: str, tmp_dir: str) -> Optional[dict]:
   225→    """Download and parse a JSON file from S3 or local path. Returns None if not found."""
   226→    if not key_exists(s3, uri):
   227→        return None
   228→    local = download_to_temp(s3, uri, tmp_dir)
   229→    with open(local, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   230→        data = json.load(f)
   231→    if is_s3_uri(uri) and os.path.exists(local):
   232→        os.remove(local)
   233→    return data
   234→
   235→
   236→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   237→# Progress State (cross-interrupt resume)
   238→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   239→
   240→
   241→def load_progress_state(s3, dst_uri: str, tmp_dir: str) -> dict:
   242→    """Load progress_state.json from dst_uri. Returns empty state if not found."""
   243→    uri = f"{dst_uri.rstrip('/')}/progress_state.json"
   244→    state = download_json(s3, uri, tmp_dir)
   245→    if state is None:
   246→        return {"completed": [], "interrupted": [], "failed": []}
   247→    return state
   248→
   249→
   250→def save_progress_state(s3, dst_uri: str, state: dict, tmp_dir: str) -> None:
   251→    """Atomically write progress_state.json to dst_uri."""
   252→    uri = f"{dst_uri.rstrip('/')}/progress_state.json"
   253→    state["last_updated"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
   254→    upload_json(s3, state, uri, tmp_dir)
   255→
   256→

   258→# Tokenizer
   259→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

**TOOL CALL [Read]:** {"file_path": "c:\\Raghu\\MyLearnings\\ERA_V4\\Capstone\\code\\p6\\P06_Tokenizer_Design_Lab\\experiments\\6_tokenizer_design_lab\\curriculum_tokenizer\\README.md"}

**TOOL RESULT:**      1→# Curriculum Tokenizer
     2→
     3→This module provides the `tokenize_curriculum.py` pipeline for tokenizing
     4→Curriculum Learning datasets from a T3 coreset index into binary shard files
     5→compatible with spdl / Megatron-style training loaders.
     6→
     7→---
     8→
     9→## Architecture — 2-Level T3 → T1
    10→
    11→The pipeline uses a **2-level architecture**. T2 is bypassed entirely.
    12→
    13→```
    14→T3 Coreset Parquet                   T1 Raw-Text Parquets
    15→(manifest / index)         →         (source documents)
    16→  chunk_id                               id  (matched against chunk_id)
    17→  t1_file_path                           text
    18→  band                                   …
    19→  domain
    20→```
    21→
    22→Each T3 coreset file is a manifest whose rows identify exactly which
    23→documents from T1 survived quality filtering. The script:
    24→
    25→1. Groups T3 rows by `t1_file_path` to minimise redundant T1 downloads.
    26→2. Downloads each T1 parquet and filters to only the `chunk_id` rows.
    27→3. Tokenizes the matching text documents.
    28→4. Packs tokens into fixed-size blocks and flushes binary shards.
    29→
    30→---
    31→
    32→## Output Structure
    33→
    34→```
    35→<dst-uri>/
    36→├── progress_state.json              ← resume tracker (updated after each file)
    37→├── manifest.json                    ← global summary (written on clean completion)
    38→│
    39→├── <coreset_stem>/                  ← named after the coreset .parquet filename
    40→│   ├── shard_001/
    41→│   │   ├── tokens.bin               (packed uint32 blocks, block_size tokens each)
    42→│   │   ├── tokens.idx               (spdl-compatible binary byte offsets)
    43→│   │   └── metadata.json            (per-shard stats + curriculum metadata)
    44→│   ├── shard_002/
    45→│   │   └── …
    46→│   └── shard_NNN/
    47→│
    48→└── <coreset_stem_2>/
    49→    └── …
    50→```
    51→
    52→### `metadata.json` fields (per shard)
    53→
    54→| Field | Description |
    55→| :--- | :--- |
    56→| `format` | `"megatron_bin_idx"` |
    57→| `idx_format` | `"spdl_v1"` |
    58→| `token_dtype` | `"uint32"` |
    59→| `block_size` | Tokens per block |
    60→| `vocab_size` | Tokenizer vocabulary size |
    61→| `pad_token_id` / `eos_token_id` | From the loaded tokenizer |
    62→| `num_blocks` / `total_tokens` | Block and token counts |
    63→| `tokenizer_hash` | SHA-256 over `tokenizer.json` + `special_tokens_map.json` |
    64→| `tokenizer_version` | Value of `--tokenizer-version` |
    65→| `band` / `band_distribution` | Dominant band and full distribution |
    66→| `domain` / `domain_distribution` | Dominant domain and full distribution |
    67→| `stage` | Training stage (`--stage`) |
    68→| `rows_input` / `rows_with_eos` / `rows_dropped` | Row-level audit trail |
    69→| `created_at` | UTC timestamp |
    70→
    71→---

   143→## Running the Tokenizer
   144→
   145→### Minimal command (S3)
   146→
   147→```bash
   148→python tokenize_curriculum.py \
   149→    --coreset-uri  "s3://your-bucket/coresets/stage1/" \
   150→    --dst-uri      "s3://your-bucket/tokenized/stage1/" \
   151→    --tokenizer-path ../tsai_131k_tokenizer
   152→```
   153→
   154→### Minimal command (local)
   155→
   156→```bash
   157→python tokenize_curriculum.py \
   158→    --coreset-uri  "dataset/final/small/t3/" \
   159→    --dst-uri      "dataset/final/small/tok_out" \
   160→    --tokenizer-path ../tsai_131k_tokenizer \
   161→    --t1-base-uri  "dataset/final/small/t1"
   162→```
   163→
   164→`--coreset-uri` accepts a single `.parquet` file **or** a folder. When a folder
   165→is given, every `.parquet` file inside it is processed as a separate batch.
   166→
   167→### Full command (all options)
   168→
   169→```bash
   170→python tokenize_curriculum.py \
   171→    --coreset-uri       "s3://your-bucket/coresets/stage1/" \
   172→    --dst-uri           "s3://your-bucket/tokenized/stage1/" \
   173→    --tokenizer-path    ../tsai_131k_tokenizer \
   174→    --t1-base-uri       "s3://t1-dataacquisition-datasets/processed_dataset/normalized_data" \
   175→    --block-size        4096 \
   176→    --shard-size-mb     512 \
   177→    --num-proc          8 \
   178→    --file-parallelism  4 \
   179→    --stage             1 \
   180→    --tokenizer-version v1 \
   181→    --drop-remainder \
   182→    --tmp-dir           /tmp/tok_tmp
   183→```
   184→
   185→---
   186→
   187→## Available Arguments
   188→
   189→| Argument | Description | Default |
   190→| :--- | :--- | :--- |
   191→| `--coreset-uri` | **[Required]** S3 URI or local path to a T3 coreset `.parquet` file or folder | — |
   192→| `--dst-uri` | **[Required]** Destination S3 URI or local folder | — |
   193→| `--tokenizer-path` | Path to a HuggingFace tokenizer directory. If omitted, looks for `tsai_131k_tokenizer/` next to the script | `None` |
   194→| `--t1-base-uri` | Base URI for T1 raw-text parquets. Full T1 path is constructed as `t1_base_uri + "/" + t1_file_path` (from T3 column). Override to a local folder for testing. | `s3://t1-dataacquisition-datasets/processed_dataset/normalized_data` |
   195→| `--block-size` | Tokens per packed block | `4096` |
   196→| `--shard-size-mb` | Target shard size in MB | `512` |
   197→| `--num-proc` | HuggingFace tokenizer subprocesses per worker (internally capped at 4) | `8` |
   198→| `--file-parallelism` | Number of coreset batch files to process concurrently (uses `multiprocessing` spawn) | `1` |
   199→| `--stage` | Training stage number — written to shard `metadata.json` | `1` |
   200→| `--tokenizer-version` | Tokenizer version string — written to shard `metadata.json` | `"v1"` |
   201→| `--drop-remainder` | Drop the tail buffer if it is shorter than `--block-size`. Default is to pad with the tokenizer's PAD token. | `False` |
   202→| `--tmp-dir` | Temporary working directory for downloads and shard assembly | System temp |
   203→
   204→---
   205→
   206→## Tail Handling and Data Integrity
   207→
   208→By default the final incomplete buffer is **padded** to exactly `--block-size`
   209→using the tokenizer's `pad_token_id`. The log will show:
   210→
   211→```
   212→[Info] Padded final block with N PAD tokens (preserved M tokens).
   213→```
   214→
   215→Pass `--drop-remainder` to discard the tail instead. The log will show:
   216→
   217→```
   218→[Warning] Dropped remainder: N tokens (approx M rows dropped/truncated).
   219→```
   220→
   221→Both paths write `rows_input`, `rows_with_eos`, `rows_dropped`, and
   222→`tokens_dropped` to `metadata.json` for a full audit trail.
   223→
   224→---
   225→
   226→## Interrupt Handling and Resume
   227→
   228→The pipeline registers `SIGTERM` / `SIGINT` handlers and polls the EC2 IMDS
   229→endpoint every 5 s for Spot termination notices. On receiving a signal:
   230→
   231→- The current T1 download finishes; no new T1 files are started.
   232→- Partially accumulated (un-flushed) blocks are discarded.
   233→- Only fully uploaded shards are retained.
   234→- `progress_state.json` is updated with the list of completed coreset files.
   235→
   236→Re-running with the **same arguments** automatically skips completed files and
   237→resumes from where processing stopped.
   238→
   239→---
   240→
   241→## Local Testing with Mock Data
   242→
   243→Use `util/create_mock_sources.py` to generate a self-contained 2-level mock
   244→dataset (T3 + T1) from real source parquets — no S3 access required.
   245→
   246→### Step 1 — Populate source directories
   247→
   248→Place real T3 coreset parquets in `dataset/source/t3_coresets/` and real T1
   249→parquets in `dataset/source/t1_rawdata/normalized_data/source=C4/`.

   256→    --t3-source-dir dataset/source/t3_coresets \

   258→    --output-dir    dataset/final
   259→```
   260→
   261→**Available profiles:**
   262→
   263→| Profile | T3 files | Rows/file | T1 files | Expected blocks |
   264→| :--- | ---: | ---: | ---: | :--- |
   265→| `minimal` | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1+ (long doc guaranteed) |
   266→| `small` | 10 | 25 | 5 | ~30 |
   267→| `parallel` | 20 | 100 | 5 | ~61 (run with `--file-parallelism 3`) |
   268→
   269→The script prints a ready-to-paste smoke-test command on completion.
   270→
   271→### Step 3 — Run the tokenizer on the mock data
   272→
   273→```bash
   274→python tokenize_curriculum.py \
   275→    --coreset-uri   dataset/final/small/t3/selected_indices_small_batch000000.parquet \
   276→    --dst-uri       dataset/final/small/tok_out \
   277→    --tokenizer-path ../tsai_131k_tokenizer \
   278→    --t1-base-uri   dataset/final/small/t1 \
   279→    --block-size    4096 \
   280→    --shard-size-mb 512 \
   281→    --num-proc      2 \
   282→    --drop-remainder \
   283→    --stage         1 \
   284→    --tokenizer-version v1
   285→```
   286→
   287→To process all T3 batch files in one call:
   288→
   289→```bash
   290→python tokenize_curriculum.py \
   291→    --coreset-uri   dataset/final/small/t3 \
   292→    --dst-uri       dataset/final/small/tok_out \
   293→    --tokenizer-path ../tsai_131k_tokenizer \
   294→    --t1-base-uri   dataset/final/small/t1 \
   295→    --block-size    4096 \
   296→    --shard-size-mb 512 \
   297→    --num-proc      2 \
   298→    --drop-remainder \
   299→    --stage         1 \
   300→    --tokenizer-version v1
   301→```
   302→
   303→---
   304→
   305→## Validating Output Shards
   306→
   307→`validate_shards.py` runs an 8-point checklist on every shard under a
   308→given output directory:
   309→
   310→1. Required files present: `tokens.bin`, `tokens.idx`, `metadata.json`
   311→2. `tokenizer_hash` matches the live tokenizer
   312→3. `eos_token_id`, `pad_token_id`, `vocab_size` match the live tokenizer
   313→4. `total_tokens == file_size / 4`
   314→5. `len(idx_offsets) - 1 == num_blocks`
   315→6. `rows_dropped + rows_with_eos == rows_input`
   316→7. `max(token_id) < vocab_size`
   317→8. `band`, `domain`, `stage` are non-empty in `metadata.json`
   318→
   319→### Usage
   320→
   321→```bash
   322→# Basic validation
   323→python validate_shards.py \
   324→    --shards-dir   dataset/final/small/tok_out \
   325→    --tokenizer-path ../tsai_131k_tokenizer
   326→
   327→# Verbose output + stop on first failure
   328→python validate_shards.py \
   329→    --shards-dir   dataset/final/small/tok_out \
   330→    --tokenizer-path ../tsai_131k_tokenizer \
   331→    --verbose \
   332→    --fail-fast
   333→```
   334→
   335→Exits with code `0` if all shards pass, `1` if any fail.
   336→
   337→---
   338→
   339→## Expected Warnings (Local Runs)
   340→
   341→When running without PyTorch installed you may see:
   342→
   343→> *PyTorch was not found. Models won't be available and only tokenizers,
   344→> configuration and file/data utilities can be used.*
   345→
   346→This is safe to ignore. The pipeline uses only the tokenizer utilities and
   347→does not load any model weights.
   348→
   349→---
   350→
   351→## Flat Global Shard Output (Updated Layout)
   352→
   353→The output layout has been updated from per-batch subdirectories to a single
   354→flat `shards/` directory with globally-continuous numbering.
   355→
   356→### New output structure
   357→
   358→```
   359→<dst-uri>/
   360→├── progress_state.json              ← resume tracker (updated after each batch)
   361→├── manifest.json                    ← global summary (written on clean completion)
   362→└── shards/
   363→    ├── shard_001/
   364→    │   ├── tokens.bin
   365→    │   ├── tokens.idx
   366→    │   └── metadata.json            ← source_file field preserves traceability
   367→    ├── shard_002/
   368→    ├── shard_003/
   369→    ├── shard_004/                   ← second coreset batch continues from here
   370→    └── shard_NNN/
   371→```
   372→
   373→Shard numbering is **global across all coreset batches** — there are no
   374→intermediate per-batch subdirectories. The `metadata.json` inside each shard
   375→retains `source_file` (the originating coreset URI) for full traceability.
   376→
   377→### Resume behaviour
   378→
   379→`progress_state.json` now also tracks `next_shard_idx`:
   380→
   381→```json
   382→{
   383→  "completed": ["uri_A", "uri_B"],
   384→  "next_shard_idx": 9,
   385→  "failed": []
   386→}
   387→```
   388→
   389→On interrupt and restart, the global shard counter resumes from where it left
   390→off. Re-running with the same `--dst-uri` is safe and idempotent.
   391→
   392→### Sequential vs parallel
   393→
   394→| Mode | Output layout | Global numbering |
   395→| :--- | :--- | :--- |
   396→| `--file-parallelism 1` (default) | `shards/shard_NNN/` flat | Yes — automatic |
   397→| `--file-parallelism N` | `shards/shard_NNN/` flat | Local per-batch (1, 2, …) — use `flatten_shards.py` to renumber globally after completion |
   398→
   399→### Validating the new layout
   400→
   401→Pass the `--dst-uri` root (the parent of `shards/`) to `validate_shards.py`:
   402→
   403→```bash
   404→python validate_shards.py \
   405→    --shards-dir   dataset/final/small/tok_out \
   406→    --tokenizer-path ../tsai_131k_tokenizer
   407→```
   408→
   409→The validator now looks for shards under `<shards-dir>/shards/shard_NNN/`
   410→instead of the old two-level `<shards-dir>/<coreset>/shard_NNN/` layout.
   411→
   412→### `flatten_shards.py` (parallel runs only)
   413→
   414→For parallel runs (`--file-parallelism N > 1`), run `flatten_shards.py` once
   415→after all batches complete to renumber shards globally:
   416→
   417→```bash
   418→# Local
   419→python flatten_shards.py \
   420→    --src-dir dataset/final/small/tok_out \
   421→    --dst-dir dataset/final/small/tok_out_flat
   422→
   423→# S3
   424→python flatten_shards.py \
   425→    --src-uri s3://your-bucket/tokenized/stage1 \
   426→    --dst-uri s3://your-bucket/tokenized/stage1_flat
   427→
   428→# Dry-run first to preview
   429→python flatten_shards.py \
   430→    --src-dir dataset/final/small/tok_out \
   431→    --dst-dir dataset/final/small/tok_out_flat \
   432→    --dry-run
   433→```
   434→
   435→The script is resumable — re-running it skips shards already present in the
   436→destination. It writes a `flatten_manifest.json` recording which coreset batch
   437→maps to which global shard range.
   438→

## Overall Assessment

The implementation is **well-structured and addresses the two core issues** we designed — the interruption integrity bug and the flat shard layout. The teammate followed the analysis documents closely.

---

## What Was Done Correctly

**`tokenize_curriculum.py`:**
- `delete_s3_prefix()` helper added (Change 4 ✅)
- `"interrupted": []` in default state (Change 2 ✅)
- Post-call `_TERMINATION_DETECTED` check in sequential loop (Change 1 ✅)
- `interrupted` entries now carry `shard_start`/`shard_end` for precise cleanup
- Resume cleanup correctly resets `next_shard_idx` to `earliest_start` across multiple interrupted batches
- `process_coreset_file()` returns `shard_end` and `next_shard_idx` — consumed correctly by the caller
- `staging_mode` flag cleanly separates sequential (flat `shards/`) vs parallel (per-batch `_staging/`) paths
- Worker function correctly overrides `dst` to `staging_base_uri` and forces `staging_mode=True`

**`flatten_shards.py`:**
- Full streaming strategy (copy N → delete N, never double-storing both)
- Resumable via existing shard detection
- `--dry-run` flag for safe pre-flight checks
- Both local (`os.rename`, O(1)) and S3 (`CopyObject + DeleteObject`) paths
- `flatten_manifest.json` records batch→shard range mapping

**`validate_shards.py`:**
- `list_shard_dirs_local()` correctly updated to `<base>/shards/shard_NNN/` one-level layout ✅

---

## Issues Found

**1. README — stale `Output Structure` section (High)**
The old per-batch subdir tree (lines 34–50) was never removed. It now directly contradicts the new "Flat Global Shard Output" section added at line 351+. Anyone reading top-to-bottom will see conflicting layouts.

**2. README — `progress_state.json` example is incomplete (Low)**
The schema shown at lines 382–387 omits the `interrupted` key that the code now actively writes. A reader wouldn't know it exists from the README.

**3. `validate_shards.py` — requires `flatten_shards.py` to have been run first for parallel output (Medium)**
The error message at line 304 says "Run flatten_shards.py first to produce the flat shards/ layout." This is correct for parallel runs, but for sequential runs the output is already flat — no flatten step needed. The message could mislead someone running sequential mode who gets zero shards for another reason.

**4. README — `validate_shards.py` usage commands not updated (Low)**
The old validation examples at lines 322–333 still reference `tok_out` as the `--shards-dir`, which still works (since the flat output goes to `<dst>/shards/`), but the example doesn't demonstrate the new expected path format after flatten.

**5. Parallel path — no `interrupted` tracking (Medium)**
If `pool.map()` succeeds but `run_flatten()` partially fails (some shards failed to move), the flattening stops with a warning but no `interrupted` state is written. On next run, `run_flatten()` is resumable, but `tokenize_curriculum.py` doesn't re-invoke flatten unless `pool.map()` completes again — which means the partially-flattened state requires manual intervention. This edge case is noted in the warning print but not handled in `progress_state.json`.

**USER:** Great findings, lets fix them one by one. Before that I have a clarification, does this initially create the folders in the old pattern and at the end come up with the new pattern? or initially itself it creates with new pattern?

**ASSISTANT:** It depends on the mode:

**Sequential mode (`--file-parallelism 1`, the default):**
Writes **directly into the new flat pattern** from the start. No intermediate staging, no flatten step. Each batch appends to `<dst>/shards/` with the global counter picking up where the previous batch left off.

```
Batch 1 runs → shards/shard_001/, shard_002/, shard_003/
Batch 2 runs → shards/shard_004/, shard_005/
...
```

**Parallel mode (`--file-parallelism N`):**
Creates the **old per-batch pattern first** in a `_staging/` subdirectory (so workers have collision-free namespaces), then flattens at the end.

```
pool.map() runs:
  _staging/selected_indices_small_batch000000/shard_001/, shard_002/
  _staging/selected_indices_small_batch000001/shard_001/, shard_002/, shard_003/

run_flatten() runs:
  shards/shard_001/, shard_002/           ← from batch 0
  shards/shard_003/, shard_004/, shard_005/ ← from batch 1

_staging/ deleted (if flatten succeeded)
```

So the `flatten_shards.py` script is **only in the hot path for parallel runs**. For sequential runs it exists purely as an optional standalone tool (e.g., if you want to retroactively flatten old output that was produced before this change).

This also means the `validate_shards.py` issue I flagged (#3) is real: for sequential runs there's no flatten step, so the error message "Run flatten_shards.py first" is misleading if validation fails for an unrelated reason.

Shall we start fixing the issues?

**ASSISTANT:** ## Issue 3 — `validate_shards.py` misleads sequential users

The relevant code is at [validate_shards.py:301-305](experiments/6_tokenizer_design_lab/curriculum_tokenizer/validate_shards.py#L301-L305):

```python
shard_dirs = list_shard_dirs_local(args.shards_dir)
if not shard_dirs:
    print(f"No shard directories found under {args.shards_dir}/shards/")
    print("Run flatten_shards.py first to produce the flat shards/ layout.")
    sys.exit(1)
```

**The scenario where this misleads:**

A user runs sequential tokenization. The run completes normally. They then run `validate_shards.py` but accidentally pass the wrong `--shards-dir` (e.g., they point it at the `t3/` folder, or mistype the path). The validator finds no `shards/` subdirectory and prints:

> *"Run flatten_shards.py first to produce the flat shards/ layout."*

That instruction is **wrong** — they don't need to run `flatten_shards.py`, their output is already flat. They simply passed the wrong directory. The message sends them chasing a non-existent problem.

The fix is a smarter message: check whether the `shards/` subdir exists at all vs. whether it exists but is empty.

---

## Issue 5 — Partial flatten failure leaves no resume state

**Setup:** You run parallel mode. `pool.map()` completes successfully (all 10 workers done, staging has 30 shards). `run_flatten()` starts moving them. It moves 25 shards, then hits an error (S3 timeout, disk full, etc.) and stops. The code at [tokenize_curriculum.py:1040-1052](experiments/6_tokenizer_design_lab/curriculum_tokenizer/tokenize_curriculum.py#L1040-L1052):

```python
if flatten_result["failed"] == 0:
    shutil.rmtree(staging_uri, ignore_errors=True)
else:
    print(
        f"[FLATTEN] WARNING: {flatten_result['failed']} shard(s) failed to move. "
        f"Staging directory preserved at: {staging_uri}"
    )
```

The staging dir is preserved (good). But what happens on the **next run**?

`progress_state.json` already has all 10 batches in `"completed"` (written after `pool.map()`). So `pending_files` is empty. The code never re-enters the parallel block. `run_flatten()` is **never called again**.

```
Run 1:  pool.map() → all 10 in completed → flatten fails at shard 26
        progress_state.json: {"completed": [all 10 URIs]}

Run 2:  pending_files = []  ← nothing to do
        flatten is never triggered
        staging/_staging/ sits there forever with 5 unflattened shards
```

The user would have to **manually** call `flatten_shards.py` to finish the job. There's no automated recovery path baked into the pipeline.

**Why the interruption bug fix doesn't cover this:** The `interrupted` tracking only exists in the sequential path. The parallel path has no equivalent — it's an all-or-nothing commit (all batches complete, then flatten). There's no state written to signal "flatten was partially done."

**The fix would be:** Track a `flatten_pending` flag or store `staging_uri` in `progress_state.json` after `pool.map()` succeeds, then check it on resume and re-invoke `run_flatten()` if it's set.

---

Both issues are **edge cases** — they won't affect a clean run. Issue 5 only surfaces in parallel mode with a mid-flatten crash, which is rare. But at 240B token scale with parallel runs on Spot instances, it's a real risk worth closing.

    52→### `metadata.json` fields (per shard)
    53→
    54→| Field | Description |
    55→| :--- | :--- |
    56→| `format` | `"megatron_bin_idx"` |
    57→| `idx_format` | `"spdl_v1"` |
    58→| `token_dtype` | `"uint32"` |
    59→| `block_size` | Tokens per block |
    60→| `vocab_size` | Tokenizer vocabulary size |
    61→| `pad_token_id` / `eos_token_id` | From the loaded tokenizer |
    62→| `num_blocks` / `total_tokens` | Block and token counts |
    63→| `tokenizer_hash` | SHA-256 over `tokenizer.json` + `special_tokens_map.json` |
    64→| `tokenizer_version` | Value of `--tokenizer-version` |
    65→| `band` / `band_distribution` | Dominant band and full distribution |
    66→| `domain` / `domain_distribution` | Dominant domain and full distribution |
    67→| `stage` | Training stage (`--stage`) |
    68→| `rows_input` / `rows_with_eos` / `rows_dropped` | Row-level audit trail |
    69→| `created_at` | UTC timestamp |

    69→from __future__ import annotations
    70→
    71→import argparse
    72→import glob
    73→import os
    74→from typing import Dict, List

    86→    # minimal: 1 T3 row, 1 T1 file.  The T1 matched row is chosen from real T1
    87→    # rows whose text is >= 18,000 chars, guaranteeing >= 4,096 TSAI tokens
    88→    # (measured ratio: ~4.29 chars/token at p10 → 18,000 / 4.29 ≈ 4,196 tokens).
    89→    "minimal":  {"num_batches": 1,  "rows_per_batch": 1,  "num_t1_files": 1, "min_text_chars": 18000},
    90→    "small":    {"num_batches": 10, "rows_per_batch": 25, "num_t1_files": 5},
    91→    # parallel: 100 rows/batch → ~16 blocks/batch; with --shard-size-mb 0.1 (6 blocks/shard)
    92→    # → shard_001 + shard_002 + shard_003 visible per batch.
    93→    "parallel": {"num_batches": 20, "rows_per_batch": 100, "num_t1_files": 5},

    97→# ── File discovery ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    98→
    99→def list_t3_source_files(t3_source_dir: str) -> List[str]:
   100→    files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(t3_source_dir, "*.parquet")))
   101→    if not files:
   102→        raise FileNotFoundError(f"No .parquet files found in {t3_source_dir}")
   103→    return files
   104→
   105→
   106→def list_t1_source_files(t1_source_dir: str) -> List[str]:
   107→    pattern = os.path.join(t1_source_dir, T1_SOURCE_SUBDIR, "*.parquet")
   108→    files = sorted(glob.glob(pattern))
   109→    if not files:
   110→        raise FileNotFoundError(
   111→            f"No .parquet files found under {t1_source_dir}/{T1_SOURCE_SUBDIR}/"
   112→        )
   113→    return files
   114→
   115→
   116→# ── Step 1: Sample T3 rows ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   117→
   118→def sample_t3_batches(
   119→    t3_source_files: List[str],
   120→    num_batches: int,
   121→    rows_per_batch: int,
   122→    min_token_count: int = 0,
   123→) -> List[pd.DataFrame]:
   124→    """
   125→    Return num_batches DataFrames, each with rows_per_batch rows sampled from
   126→    real T3 source parquets (cycling through source files when num_batches
   127→    exceeds the number of available files). Random state seeded per batch
   128→    for reproducibility.
   129→
   130→    If min_token_count > 0, only rows where token_count >= min_token_count are
   131→    eligible — used by the 'minimal' profile to guarantee each sampled document
   132→    is long enough to fill at least one 4096-token block on its own.
   133→    """
   134→    batches: List[pd.DataFrame] = []
   135→    for i in range(num_batches):
   136→        src_file = t3_source_files[i % len(t3_source_files)]
   137→        df = pd.read_parquet(src_file)
   138→        if min_token_count > 0 and "token_count" in df.columns:
   139→            df = df[df["token_count"] >= min_token_count].reset_index(drop=True)
   140→            if df.empty:
   141→                raise ValueError(
   142→                    f"No rows with token_count >= {min_token_count} in {src_file}"
   143→                )
   144→        n = min(rows_per_batch, len(df))
   145→        if n < rows_per_batch:
   146→            print(f"  [WARN] batch {i}: only {n} rows available (wanted {rows_per_batch})")
   147→        sampled = df.sample(n=n, random_state=i).reset_index(drop=True)
   148→        batches.append(sampled)
   149→    return batches
   150→
   151→
   152→# ── Step 2: Assign t1_file_path ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   153→
   154→def assign_t1_paths(
   155→    batches: List[pd.DataFrame],
   156→    t1_basenames: List[str],
   157→    num_t1_files: int,
   158→) -> List[pd.DataFrame]:
   159→    """
   160→    Add t1_file_path column to each batch DataFrame.
   161→
   162→    Row j (0-indexed within each batch) maps to:
   163→        t1_basenames[j % num_t1_files]
   164→
   165→    Distributing rows across all T1 files within every batch exercises the
   166→    groupby(t1_file_path) logic in process_coreset_file().
   167→    """
   168→    result = []
   169→    for df in batches:
   170→        df = df.copy()
   171→        df[T3_T1_FILE_PATH_COL] = [
   172→            f"{T1_SOURCE_SUBDIR}/{t1_basenames[j % num_t1_files]}"
   173→            for j in range(len(df))
   174→        ]
   175→        result.append(df)
   176→    return result
   177→
   178→
   179→# ── Step 3: Write mock T3 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   180→
   181→def write_mock_t3(
   182→    batches: List[pd.DataFrame],
   183→    profile: str,
   184→    output_dir: str,
   185→) -> List[str]:
   186→    """Write mock T3 batch files; return list of written paths."""
   187→    out_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, profile, "t3")
   188→    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
   189→
   190→    written = []
   191→    for i, df in enumerate(batches):
   192→        fname = f"selected_indices_{profile}_batch{i:06d}.parquet"
   193→        out_path = os.path.join(out_dir, fname)
   194→        df.to_parquet(out_path, index=False)
   195→        written.append(out_path)
   196→        print(
   197→            f"  T3 batch {i:02d}: {fname}  "
   198→            f"({len(df)} rows, {df[T3_T1_FILE_PATH_COL].nunique()} unique t1_file_path)"
   199→        )
   200→    return written
   201→
   202→
   203→# ── Step 4: Write mock T1 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   204→
   205→def build_t1_chunk_map(
   206→    batches: List[pd.DataFrame],
   207→    t1_basenames: List[str],
   208→    num_t1_files: int,
   209→) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
   210→    """
   211→    For each T1 filename, collect all chunk_ids that must appear in its id
   212→    column (preserving insertion order; deduplicating to guard against
   213→    repeated samples from the same source file).
   214→
   215→    Returns: {t1_basename: [chunk_id, ...]}
   216→    """
   217→    t1_chunk_map: Dict[str, List[str]] = {b: [] for b in t1_basenames[:num_t1_files]}
   218→    seen: Dict[str, set] = {b: set() for b in t1_basenames[:num_t1_files]}
   219→
   220→    for df in batches:
   221→        for _, row in df.iterrows():
   222→            t1_basename = os.path.basename(str(row[T3_T1_FILE_PATH_COL]))
   223→            chunk_id = str(row[T3_CHUNK_ID_COL])
   224→            if t1_basename in t1_chunk_map and chunk_id not in seen[t1_basename]:
   225→                t1_chunk_map[t1_basename].append(chunk_id)
   226→                seen[t1_basename].add(chunk_id)
   227→
   228→    return t1_chunk_map
   229→
   230→
   231→def write_mock_t1(
   232→    t1_source_files: List[str],
   233→    t1_chunk_map: Dict[str, List[str]],
   234→    profile: str,
   235→    output_dir: str,
   236→    min_text_chars: int = 0,
   237→) -> None:
   238→    """
   239→    Write mock T1 parquets to output_dir/<profile>/t1/source=C4/.
   240→
   241→    For each T1 file:
   242→      - Read the real T1 source parquet.
   243→      - If min_text_chars > 0, filter rows to those where len(text) >= min_text_chars
   244→        and sort by text length descending so the N matched rows are the longest
   245→        available documents.  This guarantees the tokenizer sees >= 4096 tokens
   246→        per matched row (used by the 'minimal' profile).
   247→      - Slice to exactly N + 1 rows (N chunk_ids + 1 decoy).
   248→      - Replace rows 0..N-1 id values with the N chunk_ids from T3
   249→        (all other columns — text, hash, domain, language, metadata, etc.
   250→        are preserved from the real T1 rows unchanged).
   251→      - Row N keeps its original id (not in any T3 chunk_id) — the decoy.
   252→    """
   253→    out_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, profile, "t1", T1_SOURCE_SUBDIR)
   254→    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
   255→
   256→    src_by_basename = {os.path.basename(f): f for f in t1_source_files}
   257→
   258→    for t1_basename, chunk_ids in t1_chunk_map.items():
   259→        src_path = src_by_basename.get(t1_basename)
   260→        if src_path is None:
   261→            print(f"  [WARN] No source file for {t1_basename} — skipping")
   262→            continue
   263→
   264→        n = len(chunk_ids)
   265→        print(f"  T1 {t1_basename}: {n} chunk_id(s) to embed + 1 decoy")
   266→
   267→        df = pd.read_parquet(src_path)
   268→
   269→        if min_text_chars > 0 and "text" in df.columns:
   270→            df = df[df["text"].str.len() >= min_text_chars].copy()
   271→            if len(df) < n + 1:
   272→                raise ValueError(
   273→                    f"{src_path}: only {len(df)} rows have text >= {min_text_chars} chars "
   274→                    f"but {n} chunk_ids + 1 decoy are needed."
   275→                )
   276→            # Sort longest-first so matched rows have the most tokens
   277→            df = df.assign(_tlen=df["text"].str.len()).sort_values(
   278→                "_tlen", ascending=False
   279→            ).drop(columns="_tlen").reset_index(drop=True)
   280→            print(f"    min_text_chars={min_text_chars}: {len(df)} qualifying rows; "
   281→                  f"matched row text length = {df['text'].str.len().iloc[0]:,} chars")
   282→
   283→        if n + 1 > len(df):
   284→            raise ValueError(
   285→                f"{src_path} has {len(df)} rows but needs {n} chunk_ids + 1 decoy. "
   286→                "Not enough real T1 rows."
   287→            )
   288→
   289→        # Slice to exactly N + 1 rows: rows 0..N-1 get chunk_ids, row N is decoy
   290→        df_slice = df.iloc[: n + 1].copy()
   291→        id_col_idx = df_slice.columns.get_loc(T1_ID_COL)
   292→        for j, chunk_id in enumerate(chunk_ids):
   293→            df_slice.iloc[j, id_col_idx] = chunk_id
   294→        # Row N is untouched — its original id is the decoy
   295→
   296→        out_path = os.path.join(out_dir, t1_basename)
   297→        df_slice.to_parquet(out_path, index=False)
   298→        print(f"    Written: {out_path}  ({n + 1} rows: {n} matched + 1 decoy at row {n})")
   299→
   300→
